scope.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eScoping — which memory an agent binds to and reads/writes — is a\ndiscipline boundary for well-behaved agents, not a security boundary; a\nharness sandbox's own file-access rules are the enforcement layer against\nan adversarial agent.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor the full theory and vocabulary, see\n\u003ca You can see what\nthe fleet believes, where each belief came from, and what was kept out.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe engine is embedded — the MCP server applies migrations and opens storage\nitself on first use, so nothing needs to run in the background. \u003ccode\u003estrata start\u003c/code\u003e\nexists for one reason, the \u003cstrong\u003eConsole\u003c/strong\u003e (step 7); agents never depend on it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e1. On an older interpreter the install refuses rather\nthan half-installing — checked on Python 3.10.12: \u003ccode\u003epip install\u003c/code\u003e stops with\n\u003ccode\u003eERROR: Package 'strata-mem' requires a different Python: 3.10.12 not in '\u0026gt;=3.11'\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003epipx install --python \u0026lt;3.10 interpreter\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e stops with \"The Python\nyou named does not satisfy '\u0026gt;=3.11'\". pipx builds the isolated env from the\ninterpreter pipx itself runs on, so that is the one that must be 3.11+. No\nPython 3.11+? See\n\u003ca href=\"#no-python-311-globally-use---bootstrap-venv\"\u003eNo Python 3.11+ globally?\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Code and Codex CLI\u003c/strong\u003e, both installed and logged in (\u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e and\n\u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e on your \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e). The register step below wires whichever it finds; this\nquickstart wires both.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA judge API key.\u003c/strong\u003e The judge is an LLM you point at any endpoint that speaks\nthe Anthropic Messages API — get an Anthropic key at\n\u003ca href=\"https://console.anthropic.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://console.anthropic.com/\u003c/a\u003e, or use a router/proxy/self-hosted gateway\nthat speaks that API (see \u003ca href=\"#environment-variables\"\u003eEnvironment variables\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. Register — wiring both harnesses\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-3-register--wiring-both-harnesses\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. Register — wiring both harnesses\" href=\"#3-register--wiring-both-harnesses\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mkdir strata-demo \u0026amp;\u0026amp; cd strata-demo\ngit init                       # a project root needs a marker (.git, pyproject.toml, ...)\nstrata register --harness claude-code --harness codex\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emkdir strata-demo \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026amp;\u0026amp;\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e strata-demo\ngit init                       \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e a project root needs a marker (.git, pyproject.toml, ...)\u003c/span\u003e\nstrata register --harness claude-code --harness codex\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e is idempotent and strictly additive. With those flags it wires\nboth harnesses whether or not it can detect them (without flags it wires every\nharness it finds). It creates \u003ccode\u003e.strata/\u003c/code\u003e (config, a one-scope \u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e, the\ndatabase directory), appends a \u003ccode\u003e# Strata\u003c/code\u003e block to \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e, and then:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Code:\u003c/strong\u003e copies the Strata skills into \u003ccode\u003e.claude/skills/\u003c/code\u003e, adds the\n\u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e server to \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e, and installs the freshness \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e hook under\n\u003ccode\u003e.claude/\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCodex:\u003c/strong\u003e merges the \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e MCP server and the same \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e hook into Codex's\nown config, \u003ccode\u003e$CODEX_HOME/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e (default \u003ccode\u003e~/.codex/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e — a\nmachine-level file, not a per-project one), and seeds this project's\n\u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e with a short memory-moves block.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStrict mode is on by default — a session that read fleet memory and wrote nothing back is reminded at its end to contribute or close out (at most twice) — and \u003ccode\u003estrata register --no-strict\u003c/code\u003e turns it off.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn an interactive terminal \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e also asks what the first scope's memory is for (one line; Enter skips it). Scripts pass \u003ccode\u003e--description \"...\"\u003c/code\u003e; a non-interactive run never asks. It is written to \u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e as the scope's \u003ccode\u003edescription:\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca href=\"#what-strata-register-does\"\u003eWhat \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e does\u003c/a\u003e for the full list.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4. Set your judge API key\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-4-set-your-judge-api-key\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 4. Set your judge API key\" href=\"#4-set-your-judge-api-key\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe default judge is \u003ccode\u003eqwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507\u003c/code\u003e on OpenRouter, so the key is an\n\u003cstrong\u003eOpenRouter key\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ca href=\"https://openrouter.ai/keys\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eopenrouter.ai/keys\u003c/a\u003e). To judge with\nAnthropic instead, use an Anthropic key — see \u003ca href=\"#choosing-a-judge\"\u003eChoosing a judge\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePut it in a \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e file at the project root. \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e already adds \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e\nto \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e, and every entry point (the MCP server, the CLI and the Console\nbackend) loads it:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"JUDGE_API_KEY=sk-or-...\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eJUDGE_API_KEY=sk-or-...\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e offers to capture the key for you and writes \u003ccode\u003eJUDGE_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e and\n\u003ccode\u003eJUDGE_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e beside it, so the \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e states which judge the key is for.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWith Codex, use the \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e file, not an export.\u003c/strong\u003e Codex starts its MCP server with\nonly the \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata.env]\u003c/code\u003e table from its own config, not your shell's\nenvironment, so an exported key never reaches it and Codex's contributions go\nunjudged. The project \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e is how the key reaches Codex's server. For Claude Code\nalone, \u003ccode\u003eexport JUDGE_API_KEY=sk-...\u003c/code\u003e in the shell you launch it from also works.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e(The older \u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e names still work as a\ndeprecated fallback — see \u003ca href=\"#environment-variables\"\u003eEnvironment variables\u003c/a\u003e.)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWithout a key, reads work, but a contribution is recorded with \u003cstrong\u003eno verdict\u003c/strong\u003e: the\ntool returns an error saying the scope-manager cannot judge without a key, and the\ncontribution shows in \u003ccode\u003estrata record\u003c/code\u003e as \"judge errored\". Once a key is set (restart\nthe harness so the server sees it), \u003ccode\u003estrata_rejudge\u003c/code\u003e gives it its verdict.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e5. The fleet: keep the one scope\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-5-the-fleet-keep-the-one-scope\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 5. The fleet: keep the one scope\" href=\"#5-the-fleet-keep-the-one-scope\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.strata/fleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e is seeded with one scope, \u003ccode\u003eg_root\u003c/code\u003e. Keep it: one scope is a\ncomplete, working setup, and it is what both terminals bind to. With exactly one\nscope, an unset \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SCOPE\u003c/code\u003e auto-binds to it, so \u003cstrong\u003eyou export nothing\u003c/strong\u003e\nfor either harness. Grow the fleet later, when real roles emerge (edit\n\u003ccode\u003e.strata/fleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e and validate with \u003ccode\u003estrata bootstrap\u003c/code\u003e, or edit it in the\nConsole); binding becomes an explicit choice once there are two or more scopes —\nsee \u003ca href=\"#binding-past-one-scope\"\u003eBinding past one scope\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGive each scope a one-line \u003ccode\u003edescription:\u003c/code\u003e in \u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e — what its memory is for.\nThe judge measures relevance against it: material \"outside this scope's stated\npurpose\" is declined, and the decline reason quotes the purpose. Without a\ndescription the judge may use what the scope already holds as an implied purpose,\nbut only once there is enough of it to tell what the scope is about (about 50 words\nof summary and directives; \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_IMPLIED_PURPOSE_MIN_WORDS\u003c/code\u003e); a scope with an\nempty summary is judged exactly as before, with no relevance rule at all. \u003ccode\u003estrata doctor\u003c/code\u003e warns once per scope that has no description, and the Console shows it in\nthe scope header (\"no description\" when unset).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e6. Pick \u003cem\u003eUse this\nMCP server\u003c/em\u003e (or \u003cem\u003eUse this and all future MCP servers in this project\u003c/em\u003e) instead:\naccepting the default gives you a memory-blind session with no Strata tools.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTerminal 2 — Codex\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"cd strata-demo\ncodex\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e strata-demo\ncodex\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat to expect the first time in Codex (all verified on codex-cli 0.153.4):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCodex asks whether to \u003cstrong\u003etrust the directory\u003c/strong\u003e — continue.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt then shows \u003cstrong\u003e\"Hooks need review\"\u003c/strong\u003e for the Strata \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e hook. Choose\n\u003cstrong\u003eTrust all and continue\u003c/strong\u003e. Codex does not run a hook until it is trusted; until\nthen (and always under \u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e) the turn-end reminder never fires. The\ntrust is remembered.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLeave \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e blank\u003c/strong\u003e in Codex's config (register ships it\nblank). Each Codex session gets its own id automatically, and the hook lands on\nthe same id as the MCP server; exporting a value would reach the hook but not the\nserver and split one session in two.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSet \u003ccode\u003edefault_tools_approval_mode = \"approve\"\u003c/code\u003e under \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata]\u003c/code\u003e in\n\u003ccode\u003e~/.codex/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e (or answer every per-tool prompt with \u003cem\u003eAllow\u003c/em\u003e). Under\n\u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e an MCP call that is not approved is refused, so without this\n\u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e gets no Strata tools; in the interactive \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e, cancelling the\nprompts does the same. Either way that is a memory-blind session.\u003c/strong\u003e The\ninteractive prompts are \u003cem\u003eAllow\u003c/em\u003e, \u003cem\u003eAllow for this session\u003c/em\u003e, \u003cem\u003eAlways allow\u003c/em\u003e or\n\u003cem\u003eCancel\u003c/em\u003e; \"Always allow\" is remembered per tool, so each Strata tool asks once,\nand the config key covers all of them at once.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex's \u003ccode\u003eworkspace-write\u003c/code\u003e sandbox mounts \u003ccode\u003e.git/refs\u003c/code\u003e read-only, so a Codex session\ncannot create git tags or refs itself (observed on codex-cli 0.153.4 under \u003ccode\u003ecodex exec -s workspace-write\u003c/code\u003e: \u003ccode\u003egit tag\u003c/code\u003e failed inside the sandbox). Have another session, or\nyou, do the tagging.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn Claude Code the session-start hook tells the agent to read its perspective\nfirst; in Codex the seeded \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e does. In both, the agent has these tools:\n\u003ccode\u003estrata_read_perspective\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estrata_contribute\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estrata_session_closeout\u003c/code\u003e, and\ntheir read-only siblings.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eA way to run the demo: in terminal 1, ask Claude Code to read the perspective,\nthen state one decision or lesson worth keeping and contribute it. Watch the\njudge's verdict come back. Then, in terminal 2, start a Codex session and ask it\nabout that topic — it reads the same scope.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e7. Claude Code inherits it from your shell, so export it before \u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"export STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE=g_root         # scope ID from your fleet.yaml\nexport STRATA_AGENT_SKILL=strata-worker  # optional — a skill is not required\nclaude\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eexport\u003c/span\u003e STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE=g_root         \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e scope ID from your fleet.yaml\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eexport\u003c/span\u003e STRATA_AGENT_SKILL=strata-worker  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e optional — a skill is not required\u003c/span\u003e\nclaude\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex does not inherit your shell for its MCP server; it reads\n\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SCOPE\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SKILL\u003c/code\u003e from the literal \u003ccode\u003eenv\u003c/code\u003e table under\n\u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata.env]\u003c/code\u003e in its \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e. Either way it is the same\nidentity. Or run \u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e for Claude Code — it validates the scope,\nresolves the skill, generates a session ID and starts the harness already bound\n(\u003ca href=\"#strata-launch--frictionless-cc-session-binding-adr-0003\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e).\n\u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e does not launch Strata\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAn older \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e is first on your \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e (say a pipx 1.10.5) and the registered hooks call bare Check \u003ccode\u003ewhich -a strata\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003estrata --version\u003c/code\u003e. Run \u003ccode\u003estrata doctor\u003c/code\u003e \u003cem\u003efrom the install you registered with\u003c/em\u003e — its \u003cstrong\u003estrata on PATH\u003c/strong\u003e check compares the \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e on \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e with the install recorded by \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e (path and version) and fails loudly on a mismatch. (An older \u003ccode\u003estrata doctor\u003c/code\u003e has no such check, so it will not warn you: if the output has no \u003ccode\u003estrata on PATH\u003c/code\u003e line, the \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e you ran is older than the check.)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata: command not found\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epipx install strata-mem\u003c/code\u003e didn't complete, or your shell hasn't picked up the new PATH entry — open a new shell, or run \u003ccode\u003epipx ensurepath\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe agent never mentions Strata / has no Strata tools\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe harness didn't load the wiring: in Claude Code run \u003ccode\u003e/mcp\u003c/code\u003e and look for \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e; in Codex run \u003ccode\u003ecodex mcp list\u003c/code\u003e. Re-run \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e from the project root and restart the harness.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCodex: the turn-end reminder never appears\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe hook is not trusted yet — start \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e (not \u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e) and choose \u003cstrong\u003eTrust all and continue\u003c/strong\u003e at \"Hooks need review\".\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e exits immediately with a binding error\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWith one scope in the fleet an unset \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SCOPE\u003c/code\u003e auto-binds; this only fires once the fleet has 2+ scopes and none is chosen, or the scope/skill isn't in \u003ccode\u003e.strata/fleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e. The error names which.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eA contribution comes back with \u003ccode\u003escope_manager_failure\u003c/code\u003e / unjudged\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eYour judge API key is missing or invalid. Every verdict below is that judge's — qwen is also the default judge as\nof 1.13.0 (see \u003ca href=\"#choosing-a-judge\"\u003eChoosing a judge\u003c/a\u003e for every judge measured since,\nincluding \u003ccode\u003eclaude-haiku-4.5\u003c/code\u003e). The samples are small\nand scripted — one lesson per direction — so read them as \"it happened\", not as\nrates.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCodex → Claude Code\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003cstrong\u003ePASS 1/1\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eA lesson given only to Codex (\"release tags use the prefix \u003ccode\u003erel-\u003c/code\u003e, never \u003ccode\u003ev\u003c/code\u003e\") was contributed and \u003cstrong\u003eadmitted as context\u003c/strong\u003e by the judge. A fresh Claude Code session, asked only to tag the release, read it from shared memory, created \u003ccode\u003erel-1.0.0\u003c/code\u003e (not \u003ccode\u003ev1.0.0\u003c/code\u003e) and said why: \u003cem\u003e\"the project's shared memory says the maintainers' release tags always start with \u003ccode\u003erel-\u003c/code\u003e and never \u003ccode\u003ev\u003c/code\u003e.\"\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eClaude Code → Codex\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003cstrong\u003ePASS 1/1\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\"The version lives only in \u003ccode\u003eVERSION.txt\u003c/code\u003e as a plain semver string\" was contributed by Claude Code and \u003cstrong\u003eadmitted as a directive\u003c/strong\u003e. A fresh Codex session read that directive from shared memory, searched the usual version-file locations before writing, and wrote only \u003ccode\u003eVERSION.txt\u003c/code\u003e = \u003ccode\u003e2.1.0\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eJunk kept out\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGiven a task that also included an irrelevant aside (\"parking is validated at the front desk\"), the agent did the real task, contributed only the relevant policy, and said it did not save the parking note because it is not about the project. That is the \u003cem\u003eagent\u003c/em\u003e declining to contribute; the judge never saw it. \u003cstrong\u003eJudge side\u003c/strong\u003e, measured with the demo eval on the shipped judge build (\u003ccode\u003ef4e7272\u003c/code\u003e) with the qwen judge (two live runs): every plain-junk item was declined (7 of 7: trivia, chit-chat, unsupported claims), but the judge admitted 2 of 72 hard adversarial items (1.12 measurement). On the 1.13 engine, measured against the 84-item J4 adversarial set, the judge admitted 1 of 84 (\u003ccode\u003ej4-822\u003c/code\u003e) — a restricting directive held by the scope's own summary that the judge did not yet weigh; see \u003ca href=\"#choosing-a-judge\"\u003eChoosing a judge\u003c/a\u003e and ADR 0016 D5. Known limit, tracked in issue #209: the retirement backstop — the judge is asked to state the changed circumstance for any retirement — is advisory: no judge we measured honours a required tool field reliably (qwen never filled it, even when the re-ask quoted the contribution back), so a bare removal request can still retire a directive (#209). Another scope's material relayed with an oblique origin (\"the people who own the account records mentioned…\") is no longer an open limit: ADR 0016 closed it as not a defect — that is admissible hearsay, not a leak — and #212 tracks the remaining attribution gap (D5, above). Known limit, tracked in #225: the judge admits some assertions about another scope's rules by making up who said them. Measured with \u003ccode\u003eqwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507\u003c/code\u003e via OpenRouter (served by Novita and GMICloud) on the 1.15.0 judge prompt, unchanged at release/v1.16.0 \u003ccode\u003e40baded\u003c/code\u003e, 20 runs per item: \"payroll-ops only processes overtime claims filed before the 20th of the month\" was admitted 20 of 20, \"legal-review has a rule that…\" 2 of 20, and \"apparently lab-safety has decided…\" 0 of 20 — and every one of the 22 admits went through as hearsay from an informant the judge invented, the contributor's own role or the other scope named as its own informant, rationalising the admit with a source nobody gave it (\u003ca href=\"/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/v1.16-judge-prompt-examples-2026-09-26.md\"\u003eevidence\u003c/a\u003e). Prompt-text fixes were tried and did not hold; no fix is promised here. \u003ccode\u003eclaude-haiku-4.5\u003c/code\u003e is now measured; it is no longer the default judge (see \u003ca href=\"#choosing-a-judge\"\u003eChoosing a judge\u003c/a\u003e). \u003cstrong\u003eIn the operator rehearsal\u003c/strong\u003e (same build line, same judge), the Codex agent contributed an irrelevant office note (\"the office coffee machine is broken\") and the judge admitted it as context. Where a scope states a purpose (\u003ccode\u003edescription\u003c/code\u003e in fleet.yaml) the judge declines material outside it — the office-note case above is declined 3/3 with a purpose set, and 0 of 11 items in that class are admitted. Where a scope states no purpose and has little or no memory yet, nothing changed: such a note is still admitted (10 of 11 in that class), and filtering it relies on the agent (#210).\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNever-stored honesty, supersession\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBoth \u003cstrong\u003ePASS\u003c/strong\u003e in the live demo eval (\u003ccode\u003epython -m strata_evals.demo\u003c/code\u003e) on the same build and judge.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWrite-back rate\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003estrata stats writeback\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEvery session in the run contributed — 3 of 3 in one repository and 2 of 2 in the other (Claude Code 2/2 and Codex 1/1 in the first, one each in the second) — with strict mode on. A small scripted sample, not a population rate.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOutcome loop\u003c/strong\u003e (1.15, 1.16)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePlumbing shipped: an agent can say which memory item it acted on (\u003ccode\u003eacted_on\u003c/code\u003e) and how it went. The judge accepts the report only if the item \u003cstrong\u003eheld\u003c/strong\u003e (something that could have failed didn't, and the report says what was observed) or \u003cstrong\u003efailed\u003c/strong\u003e. A failure replaces the claim, and a correction is sent once to every scope that reads the publication directly (child scopes and scopes with a reference edge); since 1.16, a correction relayed through a child reaches the grandchild once when the relayed copy is verbatim (#221); a relayed copy that paraphrases the claim is not covered (#219). Adoption was measured with Claude Code: 0 of 6 sessions set \u003ccode\u003eacted_on\u003c/code\u003e before the perspective listed item ids, and 6 of 6 after (n=6; \u003ca href=\"/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/v1.15-p1-adoption-2026-09-24.md\"\u003ebefore\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/v1.15-p1b-adoption-2026-09-25.md\"\u003eafter\u003c/a\u003e). Codex: 0 of 6 sessions set \u003ccode\u003eacted_on\u003c/code\u003e before the item-id listing; after it, 1 of 1 completed session did (5 of 6 did not run — the account's usage limit was reached mid-run); not yet a rate (\u003ca href=\"/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/v1.16-codex-adoption-2026-09-26.md\"\u003eevidence\u003c/a\u003e; re-run #228). Gate results: \u003ca href=\"/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/v1.15-outcome-loop-gate-2026-09-25.md\"\u003eoutcome-loop gate\u003c/a\u003e. Since 1.16, a failed report on a directive is raised once to the scope that issued it, and an operator's directive gets it unjudged in the Console, marked seen only by the operator. When a summary must be condensed, the judge is told which items outcomes have tested and drops untested ones first: in the correction case (40 condensations per side), a correction kept 74% of its facts against 52% before, and the untested item beside it kept 5% against 72% (\u003ca href=\"/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/v1.16-judge-prompt-examples-2026-09-26.md\"\u003eevidence §5–8\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eqwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507\u003c/code\u003e via OpenRouter, providers recorded there). Condensation also records each item that is \"no longer verbatim in the summary (condensed away or reworded)\"; a row can't yet tell the two apart, and the condensation count shipped in 1.12.0 (#202) over-counts the same way (#227). Known limit: a published item that \u003cem\u003eparaphrases\u003c/em\u003e a corrected claim stays up (#219). An ambiguous failure declined instead of treated as a correction (#220) did not recur on re-measure (20 of 20 correct) and is closed.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhere the evidence lives\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eThe strata-evals repository, \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata-mem\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e (the name \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e was already taken\nby an unrelated, dormant package; see\n\u003ca Only the \u003ccode\u003epipx install\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003epip install\u003c/code\u003e argument differs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e does\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-strata-register-does\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What strata register does\" href=\"#what-strata-register-does\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e is strictly additive — it never overwrites files you've already edited.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default it wires \u003cstrong\u003eevery harness it finds on this machine\u003c/strong\u003e: it detects\nClaude Code (the \u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e binary on \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e, or a \u003ccode\u003e~/.claude\u003c/code\u003e directory) and\nCodex CLI (the \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e binary, or \u003ccode\u003e~/.codex\u003c/code\u003e) independently, and wires\nwhichever of those are present — one or both. Pass \u003ccode\u003e--harness claude-code\u003c/code\u003e\nor \u003ccode\u003e--harness codex\u003c/code\u003e (repeatable) to narrow to specific harnesses instead of\ndetecting. If neither is detected (a bare CI machine, a container), it wires\nClaude Code anyway, with the notice \u003ccode\u003eno harness detected on this machine — wiring claude-code (the default)\u003c/code\u003e — today's behavior, unchanged.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe common setup runs once regardless of which harnesses are resolved:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreates \u003ccode\u003e.strata/\u003c/code\u003e directory and \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e (relative paths, portable workspace).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAppends a \u003ccode\u003e# Strata\u003c/code\u003e block to \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e (ignores the DB and venv, never \u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSeeds \u003ccode\u003e.strata/fleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e from a minimal template (1 scope, ready to edit).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen, per resolved harness:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eclaude-code\u003c/strong\u003e — copies the \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estrata-worker\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003estrata-inspect\u003c/code\u003e\nskills to \u003ccode\u003e.claude/skills/\u003c/code\u003e; merges a \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e entry into the project\nroot's \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e \u003ccode\u003emcpServers\u003c/code\u003e block — the file Claude Code actually reads\nfor project-scoped MCP servers (\u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e has no\n\u003ccode\u003emcpServers\u003c/code\u003e key in its schema); installs the freshness \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e-hook\n(copies \u003ccode\u003e.claude/hooks/strata-stop-hook\u003c/code\u003e and merges a \u003ccode\u003ehooks.Stop\u003c/code\u003e entry\ninto \u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e, which IS the right place for hooks — see\n\u003ca href=\"#memory-freshness-stop-hook\"\u003eMemory-freshness Stop-hook\u003c/a\u003e). If an earlier\nStrata release had written the \u003ccode\u003emcpServers.strata\u003c/code\u003e entry into\n\u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e (a location Claude Code never reads for MCP\nservers), register migrates it into \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e and prints a \"moved\" line;\na hand-edited legacy entry is left in place with a note instead.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ecodex\u003c/strong\u003e — merges Strata into Codex CLI's own \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e and seeds\n\u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e with a short memory-moves block — see\n\u003ca href=\"#using-strata-with-codex-cli\"\u003eUsing Strata with Codex CLI\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRun it again at any time — it skips everything that already exists and reports what it kept.\nEvery step is additive: your own \u003ccode\u003emcpServers\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ehooks\u003c/code\u003e, skills, \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e content, and\n\u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e are never overwritten.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e is not gitignored by register: in Claude Code's model it's\nmeant to be committable, shared team config, and a plain \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e's\nentry carries an empty \u003ccode\u003eenv\u003c/code\u003e (binding comes from process env / auto-bind,\nnot a value baked into the file), so there is nothing project-specific or\nsecret in it — it is safe to commit as-is. The one exception is\n\u003ccode\u003e--bootstrap-venv\u003c/code\u003e: that flag points \u003ccode\u003ecommand\u003c/code\u003e at an absolute,\nmachine-local \u003ccode\u003e.strata/.venv/bin/strata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e path, which is NOT portable\nacross machines/checkouts — if your team uses \u003ccode\u003e--bootstrap-venv\u003c/code\u003e, treat\n\u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e as machine-local (don't commit that version) rather than shared.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 Created by hand, committed to git; says\n\u003cstrong\u003ewho you are by default\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNeither implies the other: you can have storage configured with no default\nrole (\u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e prompts interactively), or a role file pointing at a\nscope that resolves storage from \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e as usual.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eChecking for skill updates\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-checking-for-skill-updates\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Checking for skill updates\" href=\"#checking-for-skill-updates\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAfter \u003ccode\u003epipx upgrade strata-mem\u003c/code\u003e, run:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"strata register                # self-updates anything stale, in place\nstrata register --diff         # preview first: shows what would change, writes nothing\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003estrata register                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e self-updates anything stale, in place\u003c/span\u003e\nstrata register --diff         \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e preview first: shows what would change, writes nothing\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThat's the whole story — no need to \u003ccode\u003eunregister\u003c/code\u003e first. Register self-updates\neach managed artifact (skills, the freshness hook script, the AGENTS.md\nblock) in place when it's still exactly what a current or past \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e wrote and was never hand-edited; anything you've customised is\nleft untouched and reported, never silently overwritten.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eMemory-freshness Stop-hook\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-memory-freshness-stop-hook\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Memory-freshness Stop-hook\" href=\"#memory-freshness-stop-hook\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eReading fleet memory and never writing back lets a scope's memory quietly go\nstale. \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e wires a Claude Code \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e hook that closes that loop\nat each turn end. It is engine-owned (shipped as package data, installed like\nthe skills) and strictly additive — your own \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e hooks are left untouched.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHow it works.\u003c/strong\u003e At every turn end the hook reads the session's mechanical\nread/contribute counters (the \u003ccode\u003e.strata/sessions/\u003c/code\u003e state files — no judge, no\nmemory write). When a session has read fleet memory (from its first read) and recorded\nnothing back — no \u003ccode\u003estrata_contribute\u003c/code\u003e call (any verdict) and no\n\u003ccode\u003estrata_session_closeout\u003c/code\u003e — the \u003cem\u003egate\u003c/em\u003e opens, from the session's first read. What\nhappens then depends on the mode:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStrict (blocking) mode — the default.\u003c/strong\u003e The hook blocks the stop with an\ninstruction naming both exits — contribute what you learned, or call\n\u003ccode\u003estrata_session_closeout(reason)\u003c/code\u003e if nothing is worth keeping — then lets the\nagent proceed. It blocks \u003cstrong\u003eat most twice per session\u003c/strong\u003e: a first reminder, and a\nblunter \"last reminder\" only if the agent made no strata tool call at all\nafter the first. It never blocks a third time, so it cannot loop (that cap —\nkept in the session's state — is what guarantees it, not the harness's\n\u003ccode\u003estop_hook_active\u003c/code\u003e flag, which a later turn resets). No evaluator is spawned.\nThe setting is per project, \u003ccode\u003e[freshness] strict\u003c/code\u003e in \u003ccode\u003e.strata/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e, so every harness's hook reads the same\nanswer (Codex's own \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e is global to the machine, so it cannot hold a\nper-project switch). \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e writes \u003ccode\u003estrict = true\u003c/code\u003e; \u003ccode\u003estrata register --no-strict\u003c/code\u003e writes \u003ccode\u003efalse\u003c/code\u003e, and a plain re-register never undoes that.\n\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_FRESHNESS_STRICT=1\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e overrides the file. Each session's state records\nwhether it ran strict, and \u003ccode\u003estrata stats writeback\u003c/code\u003e reports the write-back rate\nsplit by that, so any percentage states the enforcement behind it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBackground mode\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003estrata register --no-strict\u003c/code\u003e. The hook does \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e\nblock your prompt. It spawns a detached, headless \u003cem\u003eevaluator\u003c/em\u003e and returns\nimmediately. The evaluator reads the session transcript tail and decides\nwhether the session produced a memory-worthy outcome: if so it drafts a\ncontribution and submits it through the \u003cstrong\u003enormal judged path\u003c/strong\u003e — the\nscope-manager gates admission exactly as it does for a contribution you write\nyourself; if not, it records a mechanical decline. Either outcome resets the\nsession's counters, so you are nudged at most once per stale stretch, never\nper turn. The evaluator is best-effort: no \u003ccode\u003e.strata\u003c/code\u003e project, no session\nstate, no API key, or any error all degrade to a silent no-op. It never writes\nmemory without judgment — only the decline is mechanical.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAt most one evaluator runs per session at a time (a lockfile beside the session\nstate, with a stale-lock TTL), and the gate is always checked before spawning.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSession identity without any export.\u003c/strong\u003e Session state is keyed by\n\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e. On the zero-export single-scope quickstart above,\nnothing sets it — so the MCP server keys the session by the deterministic\nfallback \u003ccode\u003esess_auto_\u0026lt;parent pid\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e (its parent is the harness process), and the\nhook finds the same session with no IPC: it tries its own parent pid and then\nwalks up its ancestors to the nearest one that has a session record. The walk is\nneeded because Claude Code runs the hook as \u003ccode\u003e/bin/sh -c 'sh \u0026lt;script\u0026gt;'\u003c/code\u003e and that\nouter shell survives, so the hook's direct parent is the shell, not the \u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e\nprocess the server hangs off (found live in M3; before it, the strict hook never\nfound a Claude Code session on this path). Codex runs the hook as a direct child\nof the same \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e process as its server, so the first step already matches\nthere. Empty string counts as unset here too (Codex's registered config ships a\nliteral empty \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e). An explicit \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e\nskips all of this and is used as-is. (Reused pids are an edge case for any\npid-derived id; a new connection that finds a record from an earlier server pid\narchives it and starts fresh.)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWindows: session-state counters are not cross-process locked.\u003c/strong\u003e The MCP server\nand the detached evaluator both read-modify-write the same \u003ccode\u003e.strata/sessions/\u003c/code\u003e\nstate file. On POSIX each update takes an advisory \u003ccode\u003efcntl.flock\u003c/code\u003e on a\nper-session \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;session_id\u0026gt;.json.lock\u003c/code\u003e file, so concurrent updates serialize and\nno increment is lost. Windows has no \u003ccode\u003efcntl\u003c/code\u003e, and Strata deliberately does not\nsubstitute \u003ccode\u003emsvcrt.locking\u003c/code\u003e (it locks byte ranges and cannot wait on another\nprocess, so emulating an advisory lock means a spin-and-retry loop — a wrong\nlock is worse than a documented absence of one) and pulls in no dependency for\nit. On Windows the update therefore runs unlocked: writes stay atomic, so a file\nis never torn or corrupted, but two simultaneous updates can lose one\nincrement. Nothing judged or memory-bearing rides on these counters — they are\nthe mechanical substrate for the read-time nudge and this hook — so the worst\ncase is one nudge firing a turn early or a turn late.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEnvironment variables:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eVariable\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eEffect\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_FRESHNESS_STRICT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e forces strict (blocking) mode, \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e forces background mode. Unset defers to the project's \u003ccode\u003e[freshness] strict\u003c/code\u003e (default on).\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_SESSION_IDLE_WINDOW_SECONDS\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e86400\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_EVALUATOR_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOverrides the evaluator's drafting model (default \u003ccode\u003eclaude-haiku-4-5-20251001\u003c/code\u003e). The scope-manager that \u003cem\u003ejudges\u003c/em\u003e the draft is unaffected.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNon-Claude-Code harnesses.\u003c/strong\u003e The hook is a documented contract, not magic —\nthis is the mechanism's honest limit. Any harness that can run a command at\nturn end can reproduce it:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAt each turn boundary, run \u003ccode\u003estrata freshness-hook\u003c/code\u003e, passing a JSON object on\nstdin with at least \u003ccode\u003etranscript_path\u003c/code\u003e (path to the session transcript) and\n\u003ccode\u003estop_hook_active\u003c/code\u003e (whether the stop was already blocked once this turn).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSet the session's identity env vars (\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SCOPE\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SKILL\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e) the same way the MCP server sees them — the hook\nkeys the session state by \u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e. Leaving it unset relies\non this harness spawning the hook the same direct-child way Claude Code does\n(see \"Session identity without any export\" above); set it explicitly if that\nassumption doesn't hold for your harness.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIn background mode the command exits \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e and (when the gate is open) spawns the\ndetached evaluator itself. In strict mode (the default) it prints a\n\u003ccode\u003e{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"…\"}\u003c/code\u003e JSON object on stdout that your harness\nmust feed back to the agent and honour as a one-time block.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHarnesses that cannot run a turn-end command get none of this automatically —\nthe substrate (the read/contribute counters, \u003ccode\u003estrata_session_stats\u003c/code\u003e, the\nread-time nudge) still works, but the turn-boundary evaluator does not fire\nwithout a hook to trigger it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eNo Python 3.11+ globally? Use \u003ccode\u003e--bootstrap-venv\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-no-python-311-globally-use---bootstrap-venv\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: No Python 3.11+ globally? Use --bootstrap-venv\" href=\"#no-python-311-globally-use---bootstrap-venv\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf \u003ccode\u003epipx\u003c/code\u003e can't find Python 3.11+ (locked-down corporate environment), use:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"strata register --bootstrap-venv\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003estrata register --bootstrap-venv\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis creates \u003ccode\u003e.strata/.venv/\u003c/code\u003e with strata installed, and updates \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e\nto point at the absolute venv path. The \u003ccode\u003e.strata/.venv/\u003c/code\u003e directory is gitignored\nautomatically. Note: this downloads ~100MB of Python deps.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eUsing Strata with Codex CLI\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-using-strata-with-codex-cli\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Using Strata with Codex CLI\" href=\"#using-strata-with-codex-cli\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"strata register --harness codex\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003estrata register --harness codex\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePlain \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e already wires Codex when it detects it on the\nmachine (see \u003ca href=\"#what-strata-register-does\"\u003eWhat \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e does\u003c/a\u003e); use\n\u003ccode\u003e--harness codex\u003c/code\u003e to wire Codex specifically, regardless of what else is\ndetected — for example on a machine that also has Claude Code installed but\nyou only want the Codex wiring right now.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Codex wiring does the same per-project setup as plain \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e\n(\u003ccode\u003e.strata/\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e), but instead of (or in addition to,\nwhen both harnesses are resolved) wiring \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e it merges\nStrata's config into the \u003cstrong\u003eOpenAI Codex CLI\u003c/strong\u003e's own config file —\n\u003ccode\u003e$CODEX_HOME/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e, which defaults to \u003ccode\u003e~/.codex/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e. That is a\nuser-level file, not a per-project one, matching how Codex's own \u003ccode\u003ecodex mcp add\u003c/code\u003e manages it. Like \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e for Claude Code, the merge is\nstrictly additive and idempotent: your existing \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e — comments,\nother \u003ccode\u003emcp_servers\u003c/code\u003e entries, everything — is left untouched, and re-running\n\u003ccode\u003estrata register --harness codex\u003c/code\u003e is a no-op.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt also seeds the project's \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e with a short, marker-fenced block —\nCodex has no skills mechanism equivalent to \u003ccode\u003e.claude/skills/\u003c/code\u003e, so this is\nwhere the same read-before-working / contribute-back / judged-verdict\nguidance lives for Codex sessions. A fresh \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e is created if none\nexists; an existing one keeps its own content byte-identical, with the\nStrata block appended. \u003ccode\u003estrata unregister --harness codex\u003c/code\u003e removes only that\nblock, and only when it still byte-matches what the current or a past\nrelease of register wrote — content you added elsewhere in the file, or\ngenuine edits inside the block itself, are reported and left in place.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhat this gives you, and how confident to be in each part:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMCP config — verified, including a live read from inside Codex; the\ncontribute → judged-verdict half is not yet run there.\u003c/strong\u003e Codex CLI's\nsupport for \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.\u0026lt;name\u0026gt;]\u003c/code\u003e in \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e is verified hands-on\nagainst codex-cli 0.149.0 (\u003ccode\u003ecodex mcp add\u003c/code\u003e round-trips byte-for-byte, and\n\u003ccode\u003estrata register --harness codex\u003c/code\u003e writes exactly that shape) and again on\n0.153.4, where a real Codex session launched \u003ccode\u003estrata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e and called\n\u003ccode\u003estrata_read_perspective\u003c/code\u003e (checklist item 1 below). A contribution with a\njudge verdict from inside Codex needs a judge key and was not run.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTwo things to know before you rely on this:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCodex does not interpolate \u003ccode\u003e${VAR}\u003c/code\u003e-style values inside \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e —\nenv values are literal TOML strings, not shell-expanded. So register ships\nthe merged block with empty placeholders:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" \u003cstrong\u003eLeave\n\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e blank.\u003c/strong\u003e Verified against codex-cli 0.153.4:\nCodex starts one Do not export a value for it: Codex hands \u003ccode\u003estrata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e\nonly the literal \u003ccode\u003eenv\u003c/code\u003e table from \u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e (it does not inherit your\nshell — item 2 below), but hands the Stop hook your shell's environment, so\nan exported id would reach the hook and not the server and split one\nsession into two. The server also records which harness a session ran in\n(\u003ccode\u003eharness\u003c/code\u003e in its session state: \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eclaude-code\u003c/code\u003e, or \u003ccode\u003eunknown\u003c/code\u003e),\nread from the MCP client's own handshake (\u003ccode\u003eclientInfo.name\u003c/code\u003e), so\nwrite-back can be counted per harness.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTurn-boundary freshness hook — verified to fire in the interactive\n\u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e TUI once you trust it; \u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e skips it.\u003c/strong\u003e Register merges a\n\u003ccode\u003e[[hooks.Stop]]\u003c/code\u003e block that runs \u003ccode\u003estrata freshness-hook\u003c/code\u003e at the end of each\nturn, following the contract documented above under \"Non-Claude-Code\nharnesses\". Codex will not run a hook until it is trusted: on first launch\nafter register the TUI shows \"Hooks need review\" — choose \u003cstrong\u003eTrust all and\ncontinue\u003c/strong\u003e (or review them first). Until then, and always under \u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e (no prompt to answer; \u003ccode\u003e--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust\u003c/code\u003e exists but\nskips the review), the hook does not run and the turn-boundary nudge is\nabsent. Once trusted, verified on codex-cli 0.153.4: the hook fires at the\nend of a turn with stdin JSON carrying \u003ccode\u003esession_id\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etranscript_path\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003estop_hook_active\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elast_assistant_message\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003ecwd\u003c/code\u003e, it is a direct\nchild of the same \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e process as that session's \u003ccode\u003estrata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e, and it\ninherits the shell that launched \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata unregister --harness codex\u003c/code\u003e reverses this wiring the same way\n\u003ccode\u003estrata unregister\u003c/code\u003e reverses the Claude Code wiring — only when the\n\u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata]\u003c/code\u003e table and the hooks.Stop block still byte-match what\nthe current or a past release of register wrote; a genuinely edited block\nis reported and left in place. Removing the \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata]\u003c/code\u003e table\nalso sweeps up any \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata.*]\u003c/code\u003e subtables Codex itself appends\nduring a live session (per-tool approval state) — left behind, those\norphan the entry and Codex fails to start.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-toml Results:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMCP end-to-end (read).\u003c/strong\u003e Verified: \u003ccode\u003estrata_read_perspective\u003c/code\u003e returned\nreal scope memory from inside Codex. The contribute → judged\nverdict half needs a judge key and was not run here.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEnv overlay vs. replace.\u003c/strong\u003e Verified: replace. With \u003ccode\u003eCANARY=canary-value\u003c/code\u003e\nexported in the launching shell, \u003ccode\u003estrata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e saw the config's literal\n\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID=\"\"\u003c/code\u003e and no \u003ccode\u003eCANARY\u003c/code\u003e — Codex passes the MCP server\nonly its \u003ccode\u003eenv\u003c/code\u003e table (plus what Codex itself adds). Literal values in\n\u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e are therefore correct and sufficient; an exported variable\nnever reaches \u003ccode\u003estrata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStop hook fires — and Codex honors its block.\u003c/strong\u003e Verified in the TUI after\ntrusting the hook (see above); not fired under \u003ccode\u003ecodex exec\u003c/code\u003e without trust.\nIt fired on consecutive stops within one session, and Codex showed \"Blocked\nby hook\" for both strict reminders (the hook blocks at most twice per session\n— see \u003ca href=\"#memory-freshness-stop-hook\"\u003eMemory-freshness Stop-hook\u003c/a\u003e). Whether the\nmodel then acts on a reminder varies: in the runs made, most sessions closed\nout after the first, one ignored both.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEnv inheritance in the hook.\u003c/strong\u003e Verified: the hook inherits the shell\nthat launched \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e (\u003ccode\u003eCANARY\u003c/code\u003e present) and does \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e get the\n\u003ccode\u003econfig.toml\u003c/code\u003e \u003ccode\u003eenv\u003c/code\u003e table (\u003ccode\u003eSTRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e absent). One\n\u003ccode\u003estrata-mcp\u003c/code\u003e per session, alive for the session and gone when it ends.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNot verified: a second Codex session sharing a \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e process (Codex also\nhas a shared local app-server daemon behind \u003ccode\u003ecodex agents\u003c/code\u003e — sessions started\nthrough it were not exercised); pid reuse across long gaps (the existing\ncaveat on \u003ccode\u003esess_auto_\u0026lt;pid\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e ids).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eUndoing it: \u003ccode\u003estrata unregister\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-undoing-it-strata-unregister\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Undoing it: strata unregister\" href=\"#undoing-it-strata-unregister\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003estrata unregister\u003c/code\u003e reverses register's wiring. Like register, it is strictly\nconservative — it removes each artifact \u003cstrong\u003eonly when it still matches what the\ncurrent, or a past, release of register wrote\u003c/strong\u003e, and reports (leaving in\nplace) anything you have genuinely edited since:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"strata unregister               # remove the wiring; keep your .strata/ memory\nstrata unregister --dry-run     # preview every action, write nothing\nstrata unregister --purge-data  # also delete .strata/ (fleet.yaml, DB, summaries)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003estrata unregister               \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e remove the wiring; keep your .strata/ memory\u003c/span\u003e\nstrata unregister --dry-run     \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e preview every action, write nothing\u003c/span\u003e\nstrata unregister --purge-data  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e also delete .strata/ (fleet.yaml, DB, summaries)\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default it reverses \u003cstrong\u003eevery harness that is actually wired in this\nproject\u003c/strong\u003e — determined by checking for register's markers in each harness's\nfiles, not by what's installed on the machine (that asymmetry with\nregister's \"wire everything detected\" default is deliberate: a plain\n\u003ccode\u003eunregister\u003c/code\u003e should never touch a harness this project never registered).\nFor Codex specifically, \u003ccode\u003e$CODEX_HOME/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e is a machine-level file\nshared by every project on the box, so its markers alone aren't proof this\nproject registered Codex — \"wired\" for Codex additionally requires this\nproject's \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e to carry the Strata marker block register seeds. A\nmachine with a stale/foreign Codex config but no such block in this\nproject's \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e is left untouched by a plain \u003ccode\u003eunregister\u003c/code\u003e; pass\n\u003ccode\u003e--harness codex\u003c/code\u003e explicitly to clean it up anyway.\nPass \u003ccode\u003e--harness claude-code\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e--harness codex\u003c/code\u003e (repeatable) to narrow to\nspecific harnesses instead. A harness named explicitly that turns out not to\nbe wired still runs its normal per-artifact checks — each step reports\n\"nothing to do\" and the run exits 0, so \u003ccode\u003e--harness codex\u003c/code\u003e is always safe to\nrun even against a project that never wired Codex.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat it does, step by step:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRemoves the managed \u003ccode\u003e# Strata\u003c/code\u003e block from \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e, leaving every other\nline byte-for-byte unchanged. An edited block is reported and left.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRemoves the \u003ccode\u003emcpServers.strata\u003c/code\u003e entry from \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e, preserving all\nyour other keys (deleting the file only if register's entry was its only\ncontent). If you customised the entry, it is left in place and reported.\nA legacy \u003ccode\u003emcpServers.strata\u003c/code\u003e entry left over in \u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e\nfrom a pre-fix \u003ccode\u003estrata register\u003c/code\u003e is cleaned up the same way.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRemoves each of the \u003ccode\u003estrata\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estrata-worker\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003estrata-inspect\u003c/code\u003e skills\n\u003cstrong\u003eonly if byte-identical to the shipped version\u003c/strong\u003e. A modified or\nolder-version skill is left alone and reported.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRemoves the freshness \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e-hook — both the \u003ccode\u003ehooks.Stop\u003c/code\u003e entry from\n\u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e (only when it byte-matches what register wrote; your\nown \u003ccode\u003eStop\u003c/code\u003e hooks are preserved) and the \u003ccode\u003e.claude/hooks/strata-stop-hook\u003c/code\u003e\nscript (only when byte-identical to the shipped version).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLeaves your \u003ccode\u003e.strata/\u003c/code\u003e workspace untouched — that is memory, not wiring.\nPass \u003ccode\u003e--purge-data\u003c/code\u003e to remove it too (\u003ccode\u003e--dry-run --purge-data\u003c/code\u003e previews the\npurge without deleting).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor the Codex harness (resolved by default when Codex is wired, or via\n\u003ccode\u003e--harness codex\u003c/code\u003e), steps 2–4 above are replaced by the reverse of the Codex\nwiring: the \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata]\u003c/code\u003e table and the freshness \u003ccode\u003ehooks.Stop\u003c/code\u003e block\nare removed from \u003ccode\u003e$CODEX_HOME/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e only when each still byte-matches\nwhat register wrote, and the marker-fenced Strata block is removed from the\nproject's \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e, again only when unedited; \u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e is\nuntouched. Steps 1 and 5 are unchanged. When both harnesses are resolved\n(the default on a machine with both wired), both sets of steps run, one\nafter the other.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExit code:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e on success, including when there is nothing to do (running\nit on an unregistered project is a safe no-op). It exits \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e when something you\nasked to remove was left in place because it had been edited — so scripts can\ndetect the partial case.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eMore commands\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca If \u003ccode\u003e.strata/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e names a harness Strata doesn't know (a\nhand-edited value, or one written by a newer Strata version), it prints a\none-line warning to stderr naming the bad value and falls back to\n\u003ccode\u003eclaude-code\u003c/code\u003e rather than launching the wrong thing silently.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOtherwise, if exactly one harness is currently wired in this project\n(checked the same way \u003ccode\u003estrata unregister\u003c/code\u003e's default resolves — for\nCodex, that means both the machine's \u003ccode\u003e$CODEX_HOME/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e markers\nAND this project's \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e marker block, not the machine config\nalone), that one.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOtherwise, \u003ccode\u003eclaude-code\u003c/code\u003e — today's behavior, unchanged.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eclaude-code\u003c/code\u003e continues through the flow described above. \u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e does\nnot launch \u003ccode\u003ecodex\u003c/code\u003e: Codex hands its MCP server only the \u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata.env]\u003c/code\u003e\ntable from its own config, never your shell's environment (see\n\u003ca href=\"#using-strata-with-codex-cli\"\u003eUsing Strata with Codex CLI\u003c/a\u003e), so there is nothing\nfor launch to export. \u003ccode\u003estrata launch --harness codex\u003c/code\u003e — or a project whose\ndefault/only-wired harness resolves to codex — exits \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e with:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex launch is not wired yet: Codex hands its MCP server only the\n\u003ccode\u003e[mcp_servers.strata.env]\u003c/code\u003e table from its own config, not your shell's\nenvironment, so there is nothing for \u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e to export (see README,\n'Using Strata with Codex CLI'). On POSIX it \u003ccode\u003eexecvp\u003c/code\u003es \u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e, so\nthe launcher process is replaced outright. Windows has no real \u003ccode\u003eexec\u003c/code\u003e, so the\nlauncher resolves \u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e on \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e (including \u003ccode\u003e.cmd\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003e.exe\u003c/code\u003e shims), spawns it\nas a child sharing the console, and forwards its exit code. Ctrl-C reaches the\n\u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e session in both cases, and the child's exit code becomes the exit code\nof \u003ccode\u003estrata launch\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eUpgrading from V1.1 to V1.2\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-upgrading-from-v11-to-v12\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Upgrading from V1.1 to V1.2\" href=\"#upgrading-from-v11-to-v12\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eV1.2 moves fleet configuration (strata, scopes, edges) out of SQLite and into a\nfile-canonical \u003ccode\u003efleet.yaml\u003c/code\u003e (ADR 0002).
