memory without judgment — only the decline is mechanical.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">At most one evaluator runs per session at a time (a lockfile beside the session
state, with a stale-lock TTL), and the gate is always checked before spawning.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Session identity without any export.</strong> Session state is keyed by
<code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code>. On the zero-export single-scope quickstart above,
nothing sets it — so the MCP server keys the session by the deterministic
fallback <code>sess_auto_&lt;parent pid&gt;</code> (its parent is the harness process), and the
hook finds the same session with no IPC: it tries its own parent pid and then
walks up its ancestors to the nearest one that has a session record. The walk is
needed because Claude Code runs the hook as <code>/bin/sh -c 'sh &lt;script&gt;'</code> and that
outer shell survives, so the hook's direct parent is the shell, not the <code>claude</code>
process the server hangs off (found live in M3; before it, the strict hook never
found a Claude Code session on this path). Codex runs the hook as a direct child
of the same <code>codex</code> process as its server, so the first step already matches
there. Empty string counts as unset here too (Codex's registered config ships a
literal empty <code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code>). An explicit <code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code>
skips all of this and is used as-is. (Reused pids are an edge case for any
pid-derived id; a new connection that finds a record from an earlier server pid
archives it and starts fresh.)</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Windows: session-state counters are not cross-process locked.</strong> The MCP server
and the detached evaluator both read-modify-write the same <code>.strata/sessions/</code>
state file. On POSIX each update takes an advisory <code>fcntl.flock</code> on a
per-session <code>&lt;session_id&gt;.json.lock</code> file, so concurrent updates serialize and
no increment is lost. Windows has no <code>fcntl</code>, and Strata deliberately does not
substitute <code>msvcrt.locking</code> (it locks byte ranges and cannot wait on another
process, so emulating an advisory lock means a spin-and-retry loop — a wrong
lock is worse than a documented absence of one) and pulls in no dependency for
it. On Windows the update therefore runs unlocked: writes stay atomic, so a file
is never torn or corrupted, but two simultaneous updates can lose one
increment. Nothing judged or memory-bearing rides on these counters — they are
the mechanical substrate for the read-time nudge and this hook — so the worst
case is one nudge firing a turn early or a turn late.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Environment variables:</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variable</th>
<th>Effect</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_FRESHNESS_STRICT</code></td>
<td><code>1</code> forces strict (blocking) mode, <code>0</code> forces background mode. Unset defers to the project's <code>[freshness] strict</code> (default on).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_SESSION_IDLE_WINDOW_SECONDS</code></td>
<td><code>86400</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_EVALUATOR_MODEL</code></td>
<td>Overrides the evaluator's drafting model (default <code>claude-haiku-4-5-20251001</code>). The scope-manager that <em>judges</em> the draft is unaffected.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Non-Claude-Code harnesses.</strong> The hook is a documented contract, not magic —
this is the mechanism's honest limit. Any harness that can run a command at
turn end can reproduce it:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>At each turn boundary, run <code>strata freshness-hook</code>, passing a JSON object on
stdin with at least <code>transcript_path</code> (path to the session transcript) and
<code>stop_hook_active</code> (whether the stop was already blocked once this turn).</li>
<li>Set the session's identity env vars (<code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code>, <code>STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</code>,
<code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code>) the same way the MCP server sees them — the hook
keys the session state by <code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code>. Leaving it unset relies
on this harness spawning the hook the same direct-child way Claude Code does
(see "Session identity without any export" above); set it explicitly if that
assumption doesn't hold for your harness.</li>
<li>In background mode the command exits <code>0</code> and (when the gate is open) spawns the
detached evaluator itself. In strict mode (the default) it prints a
<code>{"decision":"block","reason":"…"}</code> JSON object on stdout that your harness
must feed back to the agent and honour as a one-time block.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Harnesses that cannot run a turn-end command get none of this automatically —
the substrate (the read/contribute counters, <code>strata_session_stats</code>, the
read-time nudge) still works, but the turn-boundary evaluator does not fire
without a hook to trigger it.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">No Python 3.11+ globally? Use <code>--bootstrap-venv</code></h3><a id="user-content-no-python-311-globally-use---bootstrap-venv" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: No Python 3.11+ globally? Use --bootstrap-venv" href="#no-python-311-globally-use---bootstrap-venv"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If <code>pipx</code> can't find Python 3.11+ (locked-down corporate environment), use:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata register --bootstrap-venv"><pre>strata register --bootstrap-venv</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This creates <code>.strata/.venv/</code> with strata installed, and updates <code>.mcp.json</code>
to point at the absolute venv path. The <code>.strata/.venv/</code> directory is gitignored
automatically. Note: this downloads ~100MB of Python deps.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Using Strata with Codex CLI</h3><a id="user-content-using-strata-with-codex-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Using Strata with Codex CLI" href="#using-strata-with-codex-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata register --harness codex"><pre>strata register --harness codex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Plain <code>strata register</code> already wires Codex when it detects it on the
machine (see <a href="#what-strata-register-does">What <code>strata register</code> does</a>); use
<code>--harness codex</code> to wire Codex specifically, regardless of what else is
detected — for example on a machine that also has Claude Code installed but
you only want the Codex wiring right now.</p>
<p dir="auto">The Codex wiring does the same per-project setup as plain <code>strata register</code>
(<code>.strata/</code>, <code>fleet.yaml</code>, <code>.gitignore</code>), but instead of (or in addition to,
when both harnesses are resolved) wiring <code>.mcp.json</code> it merges
Strata's config into the <strong>OpenAI Codex CLI</strong>'s own config file —
<code>$CODEX_HOME/config.toml</code>, which defaults to <code>~/.codex/config.toml</code>. That is a
user-level file, not a per-project one, matching how Codex's own <code>codex mcp add</code> manages it. Like <code>strata register</code> for Claude Code, the merge is
strictly additive and idempotent: your existing <code>config.toml</code> — comments,
other <code>mcp_servers</code> entries, everything — is left untouched, and re-running
<code>strata register --harness codex</code> is a no-op.</p>
<p dir="auto">It also seeds the project's <code>AGENTS.md</code> with a short, marker-fenced block —
Codex has no skills mechanism equivalent to <code>.claude/skills/</code>, so this is
where the same read-before-working / contribute-back / judged-verdict
guidance lives for Codex sessions. A fresh <code>AGENTS.md</code> is created if none
exists; an existing one keeps its own content byte-identical, with the
Strata block appended. <code>strata unregister --harness codex</code> removes only that
block, and only when it still byte-matches what the current or a past
release of register wrote — content you added elsewhere in the file, or
genuine edits inside the block itself, are reported and left in place.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>What this gives you, and how confident to be in each part:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>MCP config — verified, including a live read from inside Codex; the
contribute → judged-verdict half is not yet run there.</strong> Codex CLI's
support for <code>[mcp_servers.&lt;name&gt;]</code> in <code>config.toml</code> is verified hands-on
against codex-cli 0.149.0 (<code>codex mcp add</code> round-trips byte-for-byte, and
<code>strata register --harness codex</code> writes exactly that shape) and again on
0.153.4, where a real Codex session launched <code>strata-mcp</code> and called
<code>strata_read_perspective</code> (checklist item 1 below). A contribution with a
judge verdict from inside Codex needs a judge key and was not run.</p>
<p dir="auto">Two things to know before you rely on this:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Codex does not interpolate <code>${VAR}</code>-style values inside <code>config.toml</code></strong> —
env values are literal TOML strings, not shell-expanded. So register ships
the merged block with empty placeholders:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[mcp_servers.strata.env]
STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE = &quot;&quot;
STRATA_AGENT_SKILL = &quot;&quot;
STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID = &quot;&quot;"><pre>[<span class="pl-en">mcp_servers</span>.<span class="pl-en">strata</span>.<span class="pl-en">env</span>]
<span class="pl-smi">STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">On a fresh, single-scope fleet these empty placeholders are fine as-is —
an empty <code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code> auto-binds to the fleet's only scope (and
its <code>default_skill</code>, if it has one), the same way an unset shell env var
does for Claude Code. <code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code> stays blank either way
(see the sharpest-edge note below).</p>
<p dir="auto">Once the fleet grows past one scope, fill these in with real values
before running <code>codex</code> (or edit them per
project/session — this file is user-level, so if you work across multiple
Strata projects with Codex you'll want to keep them current, or maintain a
<code>&lt;repo&gt;/.codex/config.toml</code> override — Codex's docs list that as a read
location for trusted projects, though <code>strata register --harness codex</code>
itself only writes the global file today). <strong>Leave
<code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code> blank.</strong> Verified against codex-cli 0.153.4:
Codex starts one <code>strata-mcp</code> per Codex session, as a direct child of that
session's <code>codex</code> process, so two Codex terminals never share a parent pid.
With the id blank, <code>strata-mcp</code> and the freshness hook each derive
<code>sess_auto_&lt;parent pid&gt;</code> and land on the same id (see the checklist below),
one id per session. Do not export a value for it: Codex hands <code>strata-mcp</code>
only the literal <code>env</code> table from <code>config.toml</code> (it does not inherit your
shell — item 2 below), but hands the Stop hook your shell's environment, so
an exported id would reach the hook and not the server and split one
session into two. The server also records which harness a session ran in
(<code>harness</code> in its session state: <code>codex</code>, <code>claude-code</code>, or <code>unknown</code>),
read from the MCP client's own handshake (<code>clientInfo.name</code>), so
write-back can be counted per harness.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Turn-boundary freshness hook — verified to fire in the interactive
<code>codex</code> TUI once you trust it; <code>codex exec</code> skips it.</strong> Register merges a
<code>[[hooks.Stop]]</code> block that runs <code>strata freshness-hook</code> at the end of each
turn, following the contract documented above under "Non-Claude-Code
harnesses". Codex will not run a hook until it is trusted: on first launch
after register the TUI shows "Hooks need review" — choose <strong>Trust all and
continue</strong> (or review them first). Until then, and always under <code>codex exec</code> (no prompt to answer; <code>--dangerously-bypass-hook-trust</code> exists but
skips the review), the hook does not run and the turn-boundary nudge is
absent. Once trusted, verified on codex-cli 0.153.4: the hook fires at the
end of a turn with stdin JSON carrying <code>session_id</code>, <code>transcript_path</code>,
<code>stop_hook_active</code>, <code>last_assistant_message</code> and <code>cwd</code>, it is a direct
child of the same <code>codex</code> process as that session's <code>strata-mcp</code>, and it
inherits the shell that launched <code>codex</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata unregister --harness codex</code> reverses this wiring the same way
<code>strata unregister</code> reverses the Claude Code wiring — only when the
<code>[mcp_servers.strata]</code> table and the hooks.Stop block still byte-match what
the current or a past release of register wrote; a genuinely edited block
is reported and left in place. Removing the <code>[mcp_servers.strata]</code> table
also sweeps up any <code>[mcp_servers.strata.*]</code> subtables Codex itself appends
during a live session (per-tool approval state) — left behind, those
orphan the entry and Codex fails to start.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# what strata register --harness codex merges into config.toml
[mcp_servers.strata]
command = &quot;strata-mcp&quot;

[mcp_servers.strata.env]
STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE = &quot;&quot;
STRATA_AGENT_SKILL = &quot;&quot;
STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID = &quot;&quot;

[[hooks.Stop]]
[[hooks.Stop.hooks]]
type = &quot;command&quot;
command = &quot;strata freshness-hook&quot;
timeout = 30"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> what strata register --harness codex merges into config.toml</span>
[<span class="pl-en">mcp_servers</span>.<span class="pl-en">strata</span>]
<span class="pl-smi">command</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>strata-mcp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

[<span class="pl-en">mcp_servers</span>.<span class="pl-en">strata</span>.<span class="pl-en">env</span>]
<span class="pl-smi">STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

[[<span class="pl-en">hooks</span>.<span class="pl-en">Stop</span>]]
[[<span class="pl-en">hooks</span>.<span class="pl-en">Stop</span>.<span class="pl-en">hooks</span>]]
<span class="pl-smi">type</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>command<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">command</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>strata freshness-hook<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">timeout</span> = <span class="pl-c1">30</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Live-verification checklist.</strong> Run on codex-cli 0.153.4 (2026-09-19) in a
scratch project with a temporary <code>CODEX_HOME</code>, two concurrent sessions, with
probe wrappers around <code>strata-mcp</code> and the hook logging pid, parent pid and
environment. Results:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>MCP end-to-end (read).</strong> Verified: <code>strata_read_perspective</code> returned
real scope memory from inside Codex. In <code>codex exec</code> (approval policy
<code>never</code>) an MCP tool call is refused with "MCP tool call requires
approval" unless the server table sets <code>default_tools_approval_mode = "approve"</code>; the interactive TUI asks instead. The contribute → judged
verdict half needs a judge key and was not run here.</li>
<li><strong>Env overlay vs. replace.</strong> Verified: replace. With <code>CANARY=canary-value</code>
exported in the launching shell, <code>strata-mcp</code> saw the config's literal
<code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID=""</code> and no <code>CANARY</code> — Codex passes the MCP server
only its <code>env</code> table (plus what Codex itself adds). Literal values in
<code>config.toml</code> are therefore correct and sufficient; an exported variable
never reaches <code>strata-mcp</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Stop hook fires — and Codex honors its block.</strong> Verified in the TUI after
trusting the hook (see above); not fired under <code>codex exec</code> without trust.
It fired on consecutive stops within one session, and Codex showed "Blocked
by hook" for both strict reminders (the hook blocks at most twice per session
— see <a href="#memory-freshness-stop-hook">Memory-freshness Stop-hook</a>). Whether the
model then acts on a reminder varies: in the runs made, most sessions closed
out after the first, one ignored both.</li>
<li><strong>Env inheritance in the hook.</strong> Verified: the hook inherits the shell
that launched <code>codex</code> (<code>CANARY</code> present) and does <strong>not</strong> get the
<code>config.toml</code> <code>env</code> table (<code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code> absent). So an
exported <code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code> reaches the hook but not the server.</li>
<li><strong>One session id per session, shared by server and hook.</strong> Verified, in
both <code>codex exec</code> and the TUI, two sessions running concurrently: each
session has its own <code>codex</code> process; its <code>strata-mcp</code> and its hook have
that process as parent pid; the two sessions differ. Every session state
is keyed <code>sess_auto_&lt;codex pid&gt;</code> and carries <code>harness: codex</code>. One
<code>strata-mcp</code> per session, alive for the session and gone when it ends.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Not verified: a second Codex session sharing a <code>codex</code> process (Codex also
has a shared local app-server daemon behind <code>codex agents</code> — sessions started
through it were not exercised); pid reuse across long gaps (the existing
caveat on <code>sess_auto_&lt;pid&gt;</code> ids).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Undoing it: <code>strata unregister</code></h3><a id="user-content-undoing-it-strata-unregister" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Undoing it: strata unregister" href="#undoing-it-strata-unregister"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata unregister</code> reverses register's wiring. Like register, it is strictly
conservative — it removes each artifact <strong>only when it still matches what the
current, or a past, release of register wrote</strong>, and reports (leaving in
place) anything you have genuinely edited since:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata unregister               # remove the wiring; keep your .strata/ memory
strata unregister --dry-run     # preview every action, write nothing
strata unregister --purge-data  # also delete .strata/ (fleet.yaml, DB, summaries)"><pre>strata unregister               <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> remove the wiring; keep your .strata/ memory</span>
strata unregister --dry-run     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> preview every action, write nothing</span>
strata unregister --purge-data  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> also delete .strata/ (fleet.yaml, DB, summaries)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">By default it reverses <strong>every harness that is actually wired in this
project</strong> — determined by checking for register's markers in each harness's
files, not by what's installed on the machine (that asymmetry with
register's "wire everything detected" default is deliberate: a plain
<code>unregister</code> should never touch a harness this project never registered).
For Codex specifically, <code>$CODEX_HOME/config.toml</code> is a machine-level file
shared by every project on the box, so its markers alone aren't proof this
project registered Codex — "wired" for Codex additionally requires this
project's <code>AGENTS.md</code> to carry the Strata marker block register seeds. A
machine with a stale/foreign Codex config but no such block in this
project's <code>AGENTS.md</code> is left untouched by a plain <code>unregister</code>; pass
<code>--harness codex</code> explicitly to clean it up anyway.
Pass <code>--harness claude-code</code> or <code>--harness codex</code> (repeatable) to narrow to
specific harnesses instead. A harness named explicitly that turns out not to
be wired still runs its normal per-artifact checks — each step reports
"nothing to do" and the run exits 0, so <code>--harness codex</code> is always safe to
run even against a project that never wired Codex.</p>
<p dir="auto">What it does, step by step:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Removes the managed <code># Strata</code> block from <code>.gitignore</code>, leaving every other
line byte-for-byte unchanged. An edited block is reported and left.</li>
<li>Removes the <code>mcpServers.strata</code> entry from <code>.mcp.json</code>, preserving all
your other keys (deleting the file only if register's entry was its only
content). If you customised the entry, it is left in place and reported.
A legacy <code>mcpServers.strata</code> entry left over in <code>.claude/settings.json</code>
from a pre-fix <code>strata register</code> is cleaned up the same way.</li>
<li>Removes each of the <code>strata</code>, <code>strata-worker</code>, and <code>strata-inspect</code> skills
<strong>only if byte-identical to the shipped version</strong>. A modified or
older-version skill is left alone and reported.</li>
<li>Removes the freshness <code>Stop</code>-hook — both the <code>hooks.Stop</code> entry from
<code>.claude/settings.json</code> (only when it byte-matches what register wrote; your
own <code>Stop</code> hooks are preserved) and the <code>.claude/hooks/strata-stop-hook</code>
script (only when byte-identical to the shipped version).</li>
<li>Leaves your <code>.strata/</code> workspace untouched — that is memory, not wiring.
Pass <code>--purge-data</code> to remove it too (<code>--dry-run --purge-data</code> previews the
purge without deleting).</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">For the Codex harness (resolved by default when Codex is wired, or via
<code>--harness codex</code>), steps 2–4 above are replaced by the reverse of the Codex
wiring: the <code>[mcp_servers.strata]</code> table and the freshness <code>hooks.Stop</code> block
are removed from <code>$CODEX_HOME/config.toml</code> only when each still byte-matches
what register wrote, and the marker-fenced Strata block is removed from the
project's <code>AGENTS.md</code>, again only when unedited; <code>.mcp.json</code> is
untouched. Steps 1 and 5 are unchanged. When both harnesses are resolved
(the default on a machine with both wired), both sets of steps run, one
after the other.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Exit code:</strong> <code>0</code> on success, including when there is nothing to do (running
it on an unregistered project is a safe no-op). It exits <code>1</code> when something you
asked to remove was left in place because it had been edited — so scripts can
detect the partial case.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">More commands</h2><a id="user-content-more-commands" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: More commands" href="#more-commands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Inspect memory from the terminal</h3><a id="user-content-inspect-memory-from-the-terminal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Inspect memory from the terminal" href="#inspect-memory-from-the-terminal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata scopes              # list the fleet's strata, scopes, edges
strata summary &lt;scope_id&gt;  # curated summary (directives + context)
strata record  &lt;scope_id&gt;  # every contribution + judgment in the scope's record
strata stats writeback     # write-back rate by harness (sessions that contributed, closed out, or stayed silent)"><pre>strata scopes              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> list the fleet's strata, scopes, edges</span>
strata summary <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>scope_id<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> curated summary (directives + context)</span>
strata record  <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>scope_id<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> every contribution + judgment in the scope's record</span>
strata stats writeback     <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> write-back rate by harness (sessions that contributed, closed out, or stayed silent)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Advanced subcommands</h3><a id="user-content-advanced-subcommands" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Advanced subcommands" href="#advanced-subcommands"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata doctor                                    # diagnose config/DB/fleet/wiring/binding, offline
strata migrate                                  # apply pending SQLite migrations only
strata bootstrap --config path/to/fleet.yaml    # validate a fleet YAML (no DB writes)
strata start --reload                           # uvicorn auto-reload (dev mode)
strata start --port 8001                        # serve on a different port"><pre>strata doctor                                    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> diagnose config/DB/fleet/wiring/binding, offline</span>
strata migrate                                  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> apply pending SQLite migrations only</span>
strata bootstrap --config path/to/fleet.yaml    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> validate a fleet YAML (no DB writes)</span>
strata start --reload                           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> uvicorn auto-reload (dev mode)</span>
strata start --port 8001                        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> serve on a different port</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>strata launch</code> — frictionless CC session binding (ADR 0003)</h3><a id="user-content-strata-launch--frictionless-cc-session-binding-adr-0003" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: strata launch — frictionless CC session binding (ADR 0003)" href="#strata-launch--frictionless-cc-session-binding-adr-0003"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata launch [scope_id]</code> validates the target scope against <code>fleet.yaml</code>
directly (embedded mode — no backend required), resolves the skill from the
scope's declaration, generates a session ID, and hands the session off to
<code>claude</code> with <code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code>, <code>STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</code>, and
<code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code> already set. Run <code>strata start</code> only if you also want
the Console UI.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata launch g_arch                            # use default_skill from fleet.yaml
strata launch g_arch --skill evidence-summarizer  # override skill
strata launch g_arch --session my-sess          # override auto-generated session ID
strata launch                                   # pick from interactive list, or use .strata-role
strata launch --harness claude-code             # start this harness regardless of the default"><pre>strata launch g_arch                            <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> use default_skill from fleet.yaml</span>
strata launch g_arch --skill evidence-summarizer  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> override skill</span>
strata launch g_arch --session my-sess          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> override auto-generated session ID</span>
strata launch                                   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> pick from interactive list, or use .strata-role</span>
strata launch --harness claude-code             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> start this harness regardless of the default</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">With no <code>scope_id</code>, no <code>.strata-role</code>, and a fleet with exactly one scope,
<code>strata launch</code> skips the picker entirely — it binds to that scope and says
so, the same single-scope auto-bind rule the MCP server applies.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Which harness <code>strata launch</code> starts</h4><a id="user-content-which-harness-strata-launch-starts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Which harness strata launch starts" href="#which-harness-strata-launch-starts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata launch</code> resolves which harness to start, in order:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>An explicit <code>--harness</code> flag wins outright.</li>
<li>Otherwise, the project's recorded default — see <code>strata set-default-harness</code>
below. If <code>.strata/config.toml</code> names a harness Strata doesn't know (a
hand-edited value, or one written by a newer Strata version), it prints a
one-line warning to stderr naming the bad value and falls back to
<code>claude-code</code> rather than launching the wrong thing silently.</li>
<li>Otherwise, if exactly one harness is currently wired in this project
(checked the same way <code>strata unregister</code>'s default resolves — for
Codex, that means both the machine's <code>$CODEX_HOME/config.toml</code> markers
AND this project's <code>AGENTS.md</code> marker block, not the machine config
alone), that one.</li>
<li>Otherwise, <code>claude-code</code> — today's behavior, unchanged.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><code>claude-code</code> continues through the flow described above. <code>strata launch</code> does
not launch <code>codex</code>: Codex hands its MCP server only the <code>[mcp_servers.strata.env]</code>
table from its own config, never your shell's environment (see
<a href="#using-strata-with-codex-cli">Using Strata with Codex CLI</a>), so there is nothing
for launch to export. <code>strata launch --harness codex</code> — or a project whose
default/only-wired harness resolves to codex — exits <code>1</code> with:</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Codex launch is not wired yet: Codex hands its MCP server only the
<code>[mcp_servers.strata.env]</code> table from its own config, not your shell's
environment, so there is nothing for <code>strata launch</code> to export (see README,
'Using Strata with Codex CLI'). Start codex directly; its identity comes from
that table.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>strata set-default-harness</code> — record which harness launch starts</h4><a id="user-content-strata-set-default-harness--record-which-harness-launch-starts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: strata set-default-harness — record which harness launch starts" href="#strata-set-default-harness--record-which-harness-launch-starts"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata set-default-harness codex        # strata launch now starts codex by default
strata set-default-harness claude-code  # switch back"><pre>strata set-default-harness codex        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> strata launch now starts codex by default</span>
strata set-default-harness claude-code  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> switch back</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Writes <code>default_harness = "NAME"</code> under a <code>[launch]</code> table in
<code>.strata/config.toml</code>, read-modify-write: every other line in the file —
including a pre-existing <code>[launch]</code> table's other keys — is preserved
byte-for-byte, and re-running replaces the value in place instead of
duplicating the table. An unknown harness name exits <code>2</code> with the list of
valid harnesses; running it before <code>strata register</code> exits <code>1</code> with
<code>run 'strata register' first</code>. On success it prints
<code>default harness: NAME (used by 'strata launch')</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>.strata-role</code> — per-project default binding</h4><a id="user-content-strata-role--per-project-default-binding" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: .strata-role — per-project default binding" href="#strata-role--per-project-default-binding"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Place a <code>.strata-role</code> file at the root of a project repo so that
<code>strata launch</code> (with no positional argument) binds automatically:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="scope = &quot;g_arch&quot;
skill = &quot;code-writer&quot;   # optional; resolved from fleet.yaml if omitted"><pre><span class="pl-smi">scope</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>g_arch<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">skill</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>code-writer<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> optional; resolved from fleet.yaml if omitted</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The file is committed to git alongside the project. When you open the repo and
run <code>strata launch</code>, Strata finds the file, validates the scope, and launches
<code>claude</code> already bound — no manual <code>export</code> step needed.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Platform notes</h4><a id="user-content-platform-notes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Platform notes" href="#platform-notes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata launch</code> works on POSIX and Windows. On POSIX it <code>execvp</code>s <code>claude</code>, so
the launcher process is replaced outright. Windows has no real <code>exec</code>, so the
launcher resolves <code>claude</code> on <code>PATH</code> (including <code>.cmd</code>/<code>.exe</code> shims), spawns it
as a child sharing the console, and forwards its exit code. Ctrl-C reaches the
<code>claude</code> session in both cases, and the child's exit code becomes the exit code
of <code>strata launch</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Upgrading from V1.1 to V1.2</h3><a id="user-content-upgrading-from-v11-to-v12" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Upgrading from V1.1 to V1.2" href="#upgrading-from-v11-to-v12"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">V1.2 moves fleet configuration (strata, scopes, edges) out of SQLite and into a
file-canonical <code>fleet.yaml</code> (ADR 0002). Before upgrading, export your existing
fleet shape so it isn't lost when migration 0002 drops the SQL fleet tables:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Upgrade code</strong> — pull V1.2 (<code>git pull</code>, <code>make install</code>). The migration has not run yet.</li>
<li><strong>Export your fleet</strong> — reads the still-present V1 tables and writes <code>fleet.yaml</code>:
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata export-fleet          # writes ./fleet.yaml from ./strata.db
# or specify paths explicitly:
strata export-fleet --db /path/to/strata.db --out /path/to/fleet.yaml"><pre>strata export-fleet          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> writes ./fleet.yaml from ./strata.db</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or specify paths explicitly:</span>
strata export-fleet --db /path/to/strata.db --out /path/to/fleet.yaml</pre></div>
</li>
<li><strong>Start V1.2</strong> — applies migration 0002 (drops the SQL fleet tables) and loads the exported config:
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata start"><pre>strata start</pre></div>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata start</code> will refuse to proceed if you forget step 2: it detects a V1 fleet config in the DB with no <code>fleet.yaml</code> and exits with an actionable error pointing you back to <code>strata export-fleet</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">After step 3, edit <code>fleet.yaml</code> by hand to add per-scope skill declarations
(<code>default_skill</code>, <code>permitted_skills</code>) as needed for <code>strata launch</code> (ADR 0003).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Strata Console UI</h3><a id="user-content-strata-console-ui" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Strata Console UI" href="#strata-console-ui"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Open <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/</a> while the backend is running — a graph and list
view of the current fleet state, polling every 5 s, plus four new tabs and an
in-place operator-correction surface described in
<a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/console.md"><code>docs/console.md</code></a> and the <a href="#console">Console</a> section
below. Automatic memory writes (accept/decline)
still flow only through <code>strata.contribute</code>; the Console's own write path is
limited to the two in-person operator corrections (Replace / Retire a
directive), each behind a confirm dialog. To point the UI at a non-default
backend, edit the <code>&lt;meta name="strata-api-base" content="..."&gt;</code> tag in
<code>src/strata/_ui/index.html</code>.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Console</h2><a id="user-content-console" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Console" href="#console"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata start</code> exists for exactly one reason: to serve the Console, a local
web view of memory. Nothing else in Strata needs it running — the MCP server
and CLI read and write storage directly, with or without a backend up.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata start"><pre>strata start</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Success looks like this</strong>, run after the journey above (register, then
bind and work) — migrations already applied and <code>fleet.yaml</code> already seeded
by <code>strata register</code>, so <code>strata start</code> here just confirms the project
config and serves:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="using project config: /path/to/your/project/.strata/config.toml
  ✓ Python ≥ 3.11: Python 3.11.15
  ✓ git on PATH: git found
  ✓ write perms on data directory: /path/to/your/project/.strata is writable
  ✓ port 8000 available: port 8000 is free
  ✓ ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: JUDGE_API_KEY is set

Strata backend → http://127.0.0.1:8000
Strata Console → http://127.0.0.1:8000/

INFO:     Uvicorn running on http://127.0.0.1:8000 (Press CTRL+C to quit)"><pre class="notranslate"><code>using project config: /path/to/your/project/.strata/config.toml
  ✓ Python ≥ 3.11: Python 3.11.15
  ✓ git on PATH: git found
  ✓ write perms on data directory: /path/to/your/project/.strata is writable
  ✓ port 8000 available: port 8000 is free
  ✓ ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: JUDGE_API_KEY is set

Strata backend → http://127.0.0.1:8000
Strata Console → http://127.0.0.1:8000/

INFO:     Uvicorn running on http://127.0.0.1:8000 (Press CTRL+C to quit)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">(Run <code>strata start</code> in a directory that was never <code>strata register</code>ed — the
plain env-var-driven flow, no <code>.strata/config.toml</code> — and the first run
instead prints <code>Applied N migration(s).</code> and <code>seeded fleet.yaml from the default template; edit to suit</code>, since nothing has touched storage yet
there. A registered project never shows either line: <code>strata register</code>
already seeded <code>fleet.yaml</code>, and the MCP server already applied migrations
the first time you ran <code>claude</code>.)</p>
<p dir="auto">The <code>✓</code>/<code>⚠</code>/<code>✗</code> lines are preflight checks, run before anything else. The
judge key check is a <strong>warning</strong>, not a hard failure — if no key is set, or
it's only set in a <code>.env</code> file Strata can't find, you'll see:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="  ⚠ ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: JUDGE_API_KEY is not set. The scope-manager will not be able to judge contributions without it. Set the variable (or the deprecated ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / STRATA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) before running strata start."><pre class="notranslate"><code>  ⚠ ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: JUDGE_API_KEY is not set. The scope-manager will not be able to judge contributions without it. Set the variable (or the deprecated ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / STRATA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) before running strata start.
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata start</code> still starts in that case — memory <em>reads</em> work fine, only
live scope-manager judgments fail. Fix it by exporting <code>JUDGE_API_KEY</code> or
adding it to a <code>.env</code> file in the current directory (the older
<code>ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</code> / <code>STRATA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</code> names still work too — see
<a href="#environment-variables">Environment variables</a>); no restart needed beyond
running <code>strata start</code> again.</p>
<p dir="auto">...then open <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/ui/index.html" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/ui/index.html</a> in a browser. The Console
is local-only — it talks to the backend <code>strata start</code> just launched on your
own machine, nothing external. Stop it with <code>Ctrl+C</code> whenever — state
persists in <code>.strata/strata.db</code> and <code>.strata/summaries/</code> (or <code>./strata.db</code> /
<code>./summaries/</code> outside a registered project) and nothing else depends on the
Console being up. Alongside the memory graph and settings, it
has four new tabs, plus in-place Replace/Retire actions in the scope drawer;
see <a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/console.md"><code>docs/console.md</code></a> for the full description of each:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Turned down</strong> — every contribution the scope-manager refused for a
scope, with the reason given, plus a separate mechanical count of sessions
that read the scope and recorded nothing.</li>
<li><strong>Freshness</strong> — every active scope ranked by how many sessions have read
it since anything new was accepted, with a fleet-wide breakdown of
sessions that contributed, closed out with nothing to record, or read
silently.</li>
<li><strong>Record</strong> — one scope's full append-only contribution record, newest
first, in plain language.</li>
<li><strong>View as</strong> — exactly what an agent bound to a scope receives on a read,
broken into layers with a rough token-weight estimate for each.</li>
<li><strong>Operator corrections</strong> — replace or retire one of a scope's own
directives in person, each action behind a confirm dialog.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">The HTTP API</h3><a id="user-content-the-http-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The HTTP API" href="#the-http-api"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The Console's backend also accepts contributions over HTTP (<code>POST /contribute</code>),
with the same fields and checks as the MCP tool, including <code>acted_on</code>. The HTTP
API trusts the caller's scope; <code>acted_on</code> entitlement is checked against it.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Configuration" href="#configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Per-project: <code>.strata/config.toml</code></h3><a id="user-content-per-project-strataconfigtoml" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Per-project: .strata/config.toml" href="#per-project-strataconfigtoml"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When <code>strata register</code> has been run, the project root contains
<code>.strata/config.toml</code> with relative storage paths:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="db = &quot;.strata/strata.db&quot;
fleet_yaml = &quot;.strata/fleet.yaml&quot;
summaries_dir = &quot;.strata/summaries&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-smi">db</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>.strata/strata.db<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">fleet_yaml</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>.strata/fleet.yaml<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">summaries_dir</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>.strata/summaries<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The MCP server walks up from its current directory to find this file. When
present, it takes precedence over the env vars below — no shell exports needed
for storage paths.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>strata register</code> also records the freshness <code>Stop</code>-hook's enforcement here, and
which <code>strata</code> install ran it:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[freshness]
strict = true   # `strata register --no-strict` writes false

[install]       # written by `strata register`; `strata doctor` compares it with `strata` on PATH
strata = &quot;/home/you/.local/pipx/venvs/strata-mem/bin/strata&quot;
version = &quot;1.12.0&quot;"><pre>[<span class="pl-en">freshness</span>]
<span class="pl-smi">strict</span> = <span class="pl-c1">true</span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> `strata register --no-strict` writes false</span>

[<span class="pl-en">install</span>]       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> written by `strata register`; `strata doctor` compares it with `strata` on PATH</span>
<span class="pl-smi">strata</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>/home/you/.local/pipx/venvs/strata-mem/bin/strata<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">version</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>1.12.0<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Choosing a judge</h3><a id="user-content-choosing-a-judge" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Choosing a judge" href="#choosing-a-judge"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Strata's judging runs on a model you choose. I tried six of them against the same suites;
three completed every one, and the table in this repo
(<a href="/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/judge-baseline-2026-09-20.md">docs/evidence/judge-baseline-2026-09-20.md</a>)
shows what each did and where a cell is empty — one produced no judgments at all, one
errored on most of the adversarial set, one ran partially. A fresh install
is pointed at <code>qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507</code> through OpenRouter, the model those numbers were
taken on. If your only key is an Anthropic one, the install stays on <code>claude-haiku-4-5</code> on
Anthropic's own endpoint. That key is never sent to the router. Either way, <code>strata doctor</code>
names the judge and endpoint you are actually running. The models differ in ways the table
states plainly — how strictly each reads a scope's stated purpose, and what each costs per
run — so read it and pick. The table names what was measured, when, and on which build.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>What was measured</strong> — 2026-09-20, engine build <code>release/v1.13.0</code> @ <code>5f5bf49</code>, every
model called through OpenRouter's Anthropic Messages endpoint, one repetition of each
suite (small differences are within noise). Full study, including the models that did
not complete a run:
<a href="/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/evidence/judge-baseline-2026-09-20.md">docs/evidence/judge-baseline-2026-09-20.md</a>.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Judge (OpenRouter id)</th>
<th>Junk admission (of 90)</th>
<th>J1 accuracy (54 items)</th>

<th>$ per 90-item demo run</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>

</tr>

<p dir="auto">Junk admission counts the junk contributions (trivia, chit-chat, unsupported claims,
injection attempts) the judge admitted. J4 attack success counts the adversarial items
(spoofed authority, injected directives, laundered attribution) the judge admitted. This table states what was measured, when and
against which build; it does not rank the judges, and a judge that is not in it is
unmeasured, not worse.</p>
<p dir="auto">Two caveats that matter when you choose:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Purpose-aware relevance.</strong> With a scope purpose set (<code>description:</code> in <code>fleet.yaml</code>),
<code>claude-haiku-4.5</code> over-declined 2 of 6 legitimate operational notes (<code>wi-102</code>,
<code>wi-103</code>); qwen and gemini-2.5-flash declined 0 of 6. The other numbers above do not
show this — it appeared only in that check.</li>
<li><strong>A model id ages.</strong> Routers rename and retire models, and the default id was
measured on the date above. If it stops resolving, <code>strata doctor</code> says so and prints
the override lines. To pin the measured judge so nothing can change under you, put
<code>JUDGE_MODEL=qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507</code> in <code>.env</code>; to use another model, set
<code>JUDGE_MODEL</code> to an id your endpoint serves (ids differ by provider — OpenRouter's
Haiku is <code>anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5</code>, Anthropic's own is <code>claude-haiku-4-5</code>).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Override to any Anthropic-Messages endpoint</strong> — a router, a gateway, the Anthropic API:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JUDGE_API_KEY=&lt;a key for that endpoint&gt;
JUDGE_BASE_URL=https://your-gateway.example/api
JUDGE_MODEL=&lt;a model id that endpoint serves&gt;"><pre class="notranslate"><code>JUDGE_API_KEY=&lt;a key for that endpoint&gt;
JUDGE_BASE_URL=https://your-gateway.example/api
JUDGE_MODEL=&lt;a model id that endpoint serves&gt;
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Environment variables</h3><a id="user-content-environment-variables" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Environment variables" href="#environment-variables"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Most settings are env-var driven, prefixed <code>STRATA_</code> (the judge configuration
also accepts the shorter, provider-generic names below). When
<code>.strata/config.toml</code> is present, the first three are ignored for the MCP
server (project config wins):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variable</th>
<th>Default</th>
<th>Purpose</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_DB_PATH</code></td>
<td><code>./strata.db</code></td>
<td>SQLite path for the record store (overridden by <code>config.toml</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_SUMMARIES_DIR</code></td>
<td><code>./summaries</code></td>
<td>Directory for per-scope summary files (overridden by <code>config.toml</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_FLEET_CONFIG</code></td>
<td><code>./fleet.yaml</code></td>
<td>Fleet YAML (overridden by <code>config.toml</code>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code></td>
<td>(auto-bind)</td>
<td>The scope this session acts at. Required only when the fleet has 2+ scopes — with exactly one scope, an unset (or empty-string) value auto-binds to it. With zero or 2+ scopes and this unset, the server still starts (soft-start): every memory tool returns an actionable error until bound via <code>strata_bind</code>, or the process is restarted with this set</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</code></td>
<td>(optional)</td>
<td>The skill identifier for provenance — required only when the scope declares <code>default_skill</code>/<code>permitted_skills</code> in <code>fleet.yaml</code>, unless the scope was auto-bound, in which case its <code>default_skill</code> fills this in when unset</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID</code></td>
<td>(auto)</td>
<td>Session identifier. Absent or empty-string resolves to the deterministic <code>sess_auto_&lt;parent pid&gt;</code> (the freshness Stop hook resolves the same fallback independently — see <a href="#memory-freshness-stop-hook">Memory-freshness Stop-hook</a>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>JUDGE_API_KEY</code></td>
<td>(unset)</td>
<td>The judge's API key — for the endpoint in <code>JUDGE_BASE_URL</code> (OpenRouter by default). <code>STRATA_JUDGE_API_KEY</code> also works and wins if both are set. Works against any endpoint that speaks the Anthropic Messages API.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>JUDGE_BASE_URL</code></td>
<td><code>https://openrouter.ai/api</code></td>
<td>Points the judge at a router/proxy/self-hosted gateway — the endpoint must speak the Anthropic Messages API. <code>STRATA_JUDGE_BASE_URL</code> also works. <strong>Exception:</strong> with only an Anthropic key set (see <a href="#choosing-a-judge">Choosing a judge</a>) the default stays the Anthropic API.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>JUDGE_MODEL</code></td>
<td><code>qwen/qwen3-235b-a22b-2507</code></td>
<td>Model used by the judge (an id the endpoint serves). <code>STRATA_MANAGER_MODEL</code> is the original name and still works (wins if both are set). <strong>Exception:</strong> with only an Anthropic key set the default stays <code>claude-haiku-4-5</code>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</code> / <code>STRATA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</code></td>
<td>(unset)</td>
<td><strong>Deprecated</strong>, kept as a working fallback: used only when <code>JUDGE_API_KEY</code> is unset. On its own it keeps <code>claude-haiku-4-5</code> on the Anthropic API.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_FRESHNESS_STRICT</code></td>
<td>(unset)</td>
<td><code>1</code>/<code>0</code> forces the freshness <code>Stop</code>-hook strict (blocking) or background; unset defers to the project's <code>[freshness] strict</code>, default on (<a href="#memory-freshness-stop-hook">details</a>)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>STRATA_EVALUATOR_MODEL</code></td>
<td><code>claude-haiku-4-5-20251001</code> on the Anthropic API, otherwise the judge's model</td>
<td>Model the freshness evaluator drafts with (the judge is unaffected)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">A local <code>.env</code> file is loaded automatically for every name above.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><code>STRATA_BACKEND_URL</code> was <strong>removed in 1.5.0</strong>. The CLI inspection commands
(<code>scopes</code> / <code>summary</code> / <code>record</code>) now read the record and summary stores
directly, like every other embedded-mode consumer (ADR 0004 Decision 1) —
no backend needs to be running.</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project layout</h2><a id="user-content-project-layout" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project layout" href="#project-layout"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="README.md                # This file
