<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Running Strata in Claude Code</h2><a id="user-content-running-strata-in-claude-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Running Strata in Claude Code" href="#running-strata-in-claude-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The MCP server operates directly on the SQLite record store and summary files
(ADR 0004 Decision 1, "embedded mode"). The FastAPI backend is the Console UI
layer; running <code>strata start</code> is required only to view the UI. The agent loop
— contributions, scope-manager judgments, perspective reads — works whether
the backend is up or down.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Entitlement-scoped reads (ADR 0006 D3/D4):</strong>
<code>strata_read_perspective</code>, <code>strata_read_scope_summary</code>, and
<code>strata_read_scope_record</code> default to your bound scope
(<code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code>) when called with no <code>scope_id</code>. An explicit
<code>scope_id</code> for <code>strata_read_scope_summary</code> reaches your bound scope, its
inter-stratum ancestors, and any scope referenced by a scope on that chain
via a reference edge — at any stratum distance, per ADR 0010 (context
surface); <code>strata_read_scope_record</code> and <code>strata_read_perspective</code>'s target
stay chain-only — records audit the authority that binds you, and a
perspective composes your own chain, not a peer's. <code>strata_read_perspective</code>
itself composes those same chain-referenced scopes in as labelled,
non-binding <code>peer_reference</code> layers (<code>binding: false</code>) alongside the chain's
<code>self</code>/<code>ancestor</code> layers (<code>binding: true</code>) — a referenced scope's directives
inform the reader but never bind them. Unreferenced scopes and descendants
stay refused everywhere.
This supersedes the old HTTP-parity note for <code>strata_read_scope_record</code>:
it now loads the fleet on every call to run this check, so reading your
own scope's record while it has no rows still returns the empty record
shape (<code>{"contributions": [], "judgments": []}</code>), but a scope outside your
entitled surface raises instead of silently returning an empty record.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>For a foreign project</strong>: use <code>strata register</code> (see
<a href="#adding-strata-to-an-existing-project">Adding Strata to an existing project</a>
above). The steps below are for developing on Strata itself.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Start the backend (optional — Console UI only)</h3><a id="user-content-1-start-the-backend-optional--console-ui-only" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Start the backend (optional — Console UI only)" href="#1-start-the-backend-optional--console-ui-only"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="strata start"><pre>strata start</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The backend is only required if you want the browser Console UI at
<a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/</a>. MCP tool calls work with or without it.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Register the MCP server in Claude Code</h3><a id="user-content-2-register-the-mcp-server-in-claude-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Register the MCP server in Claude Code" href="#2-register-the-mcp-server-in-claude-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">After running <code>strata register</code>, <code>.mcp.json</code> at the repo root already
contains the correct <code>mcpServers.strata</code> entry — that's the file Claude Code
actually reads for project-scoped MCP servers, not <code>.claude/settings.json</code>.
<strong>This applies to the Strata repo itself too</strong>: without a discoverable
<code>.strata/config.toml</code> the MCP server still starts (soft-start, ADR 0005 D5
dated addendum), but every memory tool stays gated with an actionable error
naming the missing config until one exists and the server is restarted — so
for developing on Strata run <code>strata register</code> once from the repo root — it
is strictly additive, and the created <code>.strata/</code> workspace is gitignored.
The <code>.mcp.json</code> entry it merges is:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;mcpServers&quot;: {
    &quot;strata&quot;: {
      &quot;command&quot;: &quot;strata-mcp&quot;,
      &quot;env&quot;: {}
    }
  }
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"mcpServers"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"strata"</span>: {
      <span class="pl-ent">"command"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>strata-mcp<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
      <span class="pl-ent">"env"</span>: {}
    }
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The seeded fleet has one scope, so an unset <code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code> auto-binds
to it (see <a href="#binding-past-one-scope">Binding past one scope</a>) — nothing to export
before launching <code>claude</code>. Once you add scopes, set <code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code> and
<code>STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</code> in the shell before launching. Storage paths are read
from <code>.strata/config.toml</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>STRATA_AGENT_SKILL</code> is a skill identifier recorded in provenance and
<strong>validated against the scope's <code>permitted_skills</code></strong> in <code>fleet.yaml</code> (when
that list is set and there's a mismatch, the server still starts — soft-start
— but every memory tool stays gated until bound to a permitted skill via
<code>strata_bind</code>). It does not select a Claude Code skill file — <strong>the same
generic CC skill (<code>strata-worker</code>) works for any role at any scope</strong>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Invoke a skill</h3><a id="user-content-3-invoke-a-skill" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Invoke a skill" href="#3-invoke-a-skill"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The repo ships three CC skills under <code>.claude/skills/</code>:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Skill</th>
<th>What it does</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>/strata</code></td>
<td>First-time orientation: shows the fleet, helps you pick a role, points you to the next skill. Use once.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>/strata-worker</code></td>
<td>Binds the current CC session as a worker at <code>STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE</code>. Reads the perspective, contributes observations as <code>context</code>, contributes decisions as <code>directive</code>, cites memory back to you. <strong>The main skill you'll use.</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>/strata-inspect</code></td>
<td>Read-only browser. Use when you want to look around without acting.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Worked example (multi-session)</h3><a id="user-content-4-worked-example-multi-session" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Worked example (multi-session)" href="#4-worked-example-multi-session"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Three terminals, three different roles, one shared Strata:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Terminal 1 — backend
strata start

# Terminal 2 — architect (skills must be permitted for the scope in fleet.yaml;
# the dev-team template permits code-writer + evidence-summarizer here)
STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE=g_arch     STRATA_AGENT_SKILL=code-writer   \
STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID=sess_arch  claude
# Then in the CC session:  /strata-worker

# Terminal 3 — backend developer
STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE=g_backend  STRATA_AGENT_SKILL=code-writer   \
STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID=sess_dev   claude
# Then in the CC session:  /strata-worker"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Terminal 1 — backend</span>
strata start

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Terminal 2 — architect (skills must be permitted for the scope in fleet.yaml;</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> the dev-team template permits code-writer + evidence-summarizer here)</span>
STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE=g_arch     STRATA_AGENT_SKILL=code-writer   \
STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID=sess_arch  claude
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Then in the CC session:  /strata-worker</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Terminal 3 — backend developer</span>
STRATA_AGENT_SCOPE=g_backend  STRATA_AGENT_SKILL=code-writer   \
STRATA_AGENT_SESSION_ID=sess_dev   claude
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Then in the CC session:  /strata-worker</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Each session contributes to the same backend. The developer captures
implementation patterns as <code>context</code>; the architect ratifies recurring
patterns into <code>directive</code>s that bind everyone below. Watch the state
evolve in <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/</a> (the Console UI) or run <code>strata summary g_arch</code> from a fourth terminal.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Several terminals on one machine are safe, even without the backend
running.</strong> Every <code>claude</code> session above talks to its own <code>strata-mcp</code>
process, and two of those processes — or a process and the optional Console
backend — can end up contributing to the same scope at the same time. Each
holds its own per-scope lock file under <code>.strata/.locks/</code> for the moment it
takes to append a contribution and, separately, for the moment it takes to
judge one — the OS enforces that only one process holds a given lock file at
a time, so two contributions to the same scope can never interleave and
leave the summary out of sync with the record (ADR 0012). Nothing extra to
start or configure: the lock files are created on demand next to your
<code>strata.db</code>, so this holds whether or not <code>strata start</code> is running.
(Windows: <code>strata-mcp</code> still serializes concurrent contributions inside one
process; across processes it does not — see ADR 0012.)</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Developing</h2><a id="user-content-developing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Developing" href="#developing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Working on Strata itself (not just using it) needs a clone of this repo,
not a <code>pipx install</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/oren198/Strata.git
cd Strata
make install        # editable install + dev extras (pip install -e &quot;.[dev]&quot;)"><pre>git clone https://github.com/oren198/Strata.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> Strata
make install        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> editable install + dev extras (pip install -e ".[dev]")</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you prefer an isolated virtual environment first:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="python3 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate   # Windows: .venv\Scripts\activate
make install"><pre>python3 -m venv .venv
<span class="pl-c1">source</span> .venv/bin/activate   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Windows: .venv\Scripts\activate</span>
make install</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run the tests</h3><a id="user-content-run-the-tests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run the tests" href="#run-the-tests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="make test         # full suite (scope-manager mocked — no API key needed)
make smoke        # end-to-end smoke (bootstrap → contribute → summary)
make lint         # ruff check + ruff format --check"><pre>make <span class="pl-c1">test</span>         <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> full suite (scope-manager mocked — no API key needed)</span>
make smoke        <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> end-to-end smoke (bootstrap → contribute → summary)</span>
make lint         <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> ruff check + ruff format --check</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To run the (skipped-by-default) integration test that hits the real
Anthropic API:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="STRATA_RUN_INTEGRATION=1 ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=... pytest tests/test_scope_manager.py -v"><pre>STRATA_RUN_INTEGRATION=1 ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=... pytest tests/test_scope_manager.py -v</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The original <code>make</code> targets (<code>make migrate</code>, <code>make bootstrap</code>, <code>make run</code>,
<code>make test</code>, <code>make lint</code>, <code>make smoke</code>) all still work against a repo clone
and are the fastest path when hacking on Strata itself.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Git workflow</h2><a id="user-content-git-workflow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Git workflow" href="#git-workflow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>main</code> — the last verified version of Strata.</li>
<li><code>dev</code> — the integration branch. All feature work merges here first.</li>
<li><code>feature/*</code> — branched from <code>dev</code>, merged back into <code>dev</code> via PR.</li>
<li>Releases are PRs from <code>dev</code> → <code>main</code>.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Architecture decisions</h2><a id="user-content-architecture-decisions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Architecture decisions" href="#architecture-decisions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">ADRs live under <code>docs/adr/</code>. Each captures a hard-to-reverse decision with
context, alternatives, and consequences. The future direction —
principles plus the next horizons — is in <a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/ROADMAP.md"><code>docs/ROADMAP.md</code></a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Current ADRs:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/adr/0001-v1-architecture.md">0001 — V1 architecture</a>: local Python
backend, SQLite + markdown storage, Claude Code as the initial agent
runtime, scope-manager hosted as backend-spawned LLM judgment calls
(see <a href="#environment-variables">Environment variables</a> for today's
provider-generic judge configuration).</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/adr/0002-fleet-config-source-of-truth.md">0002 — Fleet config source of truth</a>:
<code>fleet.yaml</code> is canonical; SQLite holds only contributions and judgments;
scope lifecycle (<code>active</code>/<code>archived</code>); per-scope skill declarations.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/adr/0003-strata-launch-cc-binding.md">0003 — <code>strata launch</code> CC binding</a>:
frictionless <code>(scope, skill, session_id)</code> binding via a single CLI command
that validates, resolves, and <code>execvp</code>s <code>claude</code>.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/adr/0004-h2-foundations.md">0004 — H2 foundations</a>: embedded mode
(MCP server direct-store access), manager composition, lazy refresh, bounded
summaries.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/oren198/Strata/blob/main/docs/adr/0005-brownfield-install.md">0005 — Brownfield install</a>: <code>strata register</code>
two-command onboarding, per-project <code>.strata/config.toml</code> discovery, <code>strata-mcp</code>
console script, skills as package data, honest provenance enforcement.</li>
</ul>
